制造业PMI 非制造业PMI
10月份制造业PMI季节性回落至收缩区间（49.3）。分项来看，价格分项高位回落但仍维持扩
张，购进和出厂价格分别为  56.8（-4.0）和51.7（-2.3）  ，二者差缩至  5.1pcts  ，中下游利润挤
压边际缓解。生产指数  49.8（-1.9）  、新订单  49.4（-1.1）  ，产需缺口由上月1.2pcts收窄
至  0.4pcts  ，两者均落入收缩区间，指向企业根据订单变化主动下调产出节奏。新出口订单回落

至  48.7（-1.3）  ，不过仍高于过去五年同期水平，显示外需支撑边际减弱但仍具有相对韧性。原
材料库存  48.4（+0.2）  、产成品库存  47.5（-0.1）  ，叠加采购量  48.9  （-2.1）与进口  47.0（-
2.2）  回落，反映订单走弱背景下企业“以销定产”。 高技术  51.3  与装备  50.6  维持扩张，对冲总
量回落，而消费品  48.8  、高耗能  47.3  拖累更加明显，“K型分化”扩大。大型企业  50.2  相对稳
定，中小企业收缩进一步深化，分化加剧。非制造业商务活动指数  49.7（-0.5）  ，服务业  50.0
（-0.2）  ，建筑业  48.0（-2.3）未能延续9月的回升态势，“银十”未形成有效托底，建筑开工
进度偏慢。总体来看，10月回落幅度与历史季节性大致相当，短期扰动（长假与前期备货透支）
叠加需求接续不足是主因，四季度修复仍依赖财政资金加快转化为实物工作量与内需改善落地。

Document 028: 20261005_投資週報_製造業加速擴張，AI需求推動市場

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本

意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 05 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國製造業維持擴張，成本壓力升高：9 月 ISM 製造業調查顯示，美國製造業連續第九個月
位於擴張區間，新訂單與就業分項均見改善，反映接單與用人需求仍有支撐。然而，價格指數
由 8 月的 71.1 升至 9 月的 77.9，能源、金屬及關稅相關成本上升，加上供應商交貨偏慢，企
業面臨更大成本壓力 。 後續須觀察企業能否轉嫁成本 ， 以及通膨黏性對聯準會政策判斷的影響 。
◼ 日本短觀顯示製造業改善、服務業略緩：日本銀行 10 月 1 日公布的 9 月短觀顯示，大型製造

業景氣判斷指數由 6 月的＋22 升至＋24，大型非製造業則由＋37 降至＋35。企業預估 12 月
兩項指數分別回落至＋21 與＋30，顯示現況仍有支撐，後續看法則偏審慎。製造業改善有助
支撐日股獲利預期，但服務業放緩及日銀政策路徑仍須持續觀察。
◼ 台灣製造業活動加速擴張，AI 需求續強：10 月 1 日公布的製造業調查顯示，台灣 9 月製造業
活動擴張速度創 2021 年 8 月以來最快，AI 相關應用需求仍是重要推力。國發會 9 月 29 日公
布的 8 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，與 7 月持平，領先及同時指標皆續
升。科技供應鏈景氣仍有支撐，但成長動能能否擴及其他產業，仍是觀察後續景氣的重點。
市場展望
◼ AI 需求持續擴張，關注財報季表現： AI 基礎建設需求未受利率環境影響，而是持續擴張，帶
動光通訊、CCL、ABF 載板 、記憶體、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供應鏈維持強勁成長，市場表
現由全面上漲轉向結構性分化。隨新一代 AI 伺服器放量、CSP 資本支出維持高檔，具供給優

勢、定價能力及訂單能見度延伸至 2027 年的龍頭廠商仍可望受惠。10 月中旬起陸續公布的
第三季財報，若結果持續表現亮眼，可望為市場再添動能，屆時具 AI 供應鏈題材的台股與日
股亦可望受惠。
◼ 債券佈局聚焦 5~10 年期美國公債及投等債：雖然貨幣政策風險 下降有助於穩定長期通膨預
期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長
天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率環境下，5~10 年期債券較能兼顧票息收益與存續
期間風險管理。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級公司債指數利差約 80-90 個基點，雖然利差
偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供具吸引力的絕對收益水準，因此現階段5~10 年期美國公債及
投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

Document 031: 20261019_投資週報_通膨升溫增添波動，台積財報展現韌性

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同
意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元 ； 公司同時將2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%， 反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險 ， 油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力 ：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施 ，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變 ， 但短線波動勢將加劇 ：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫 、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期 ， 可見AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象 ，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性 ， 加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加 、 期限溢酬及利率波動影響 ， 因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)

3Q Result Miss, Expecting Recovery in 4Q26
Delta’s 3Q earnings and GM were below expectation due to component shortages
and delays in data center deployment. GM declined 1.6ppts QoQ to 34.1%,
primarily reflecting inventory provisions equivalent to 1.7% of revenue, driven by
supply chain mismatches, customer delivery postponements, and product
specification adjustments. Nevertheless, OPM remained resilient at 16.6%, broadly

flat QoQ and YoY, supported by operating leverage.
Management indicated that AI-related businesses across its four divisions now
account for approximately 50% of consolidated revenue, highlighting Delta’s
increasing exposure to AI infrastructure investment. Management remains
confident in Delta’s competitive positioning, particularly its integrated capabilities
spanning power conversion, power distribution, thermal management, and
infrastructure solutions. Although the industry continues to face component

shortages, especially for certain power semiconductor devices, Delta believes its
supply chain management capabilities compare favorably to competitors. The
company also plans to establish a major manufacturing facility in Penang,
Malaysia, leveraging the region’s established infrastructure supply chain and
engineering talent.
Figure 1. Delta - 3Q26 earnings review
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Company Reports, Citi Research Estimates, Bloomberg consensus

HVDC Adoption and SOFC Commercialization Create
Additional Long- T erm Growth Opportunities
Delta expects the transition toward high-voltage DC power architecture to
accelerate as AI rack power density continues to increase. The company began
shipping ±400V HVDC solutions in 3Q26, while NVIDIA is promoting 800VDC
architectures for next-generation AI infrastructure. Management believes HVDC
adoption will eventually become widespread given its potential to improve power

conversion efficiency and reduce distribution losses, although standardization,
installation requirements, and shortages of qualified electrical workers could
constrain the near-term rollout. Beyond power conversion, Delta is developing
solid oxide fuel cell (SOFC) solutions to address growing demand for distributed
power generation. The company plans to begin commercial production in 2027,
with an initial manufacturing line providing approximately 100MW of annual

capacity, primarily supporting customer qualification and pilot installations. Initial

customers include domestic utility and industrial users, alongside several overseas

4Q26 Recovery Expected as Inventory Normalizes, with
Positive Growth Outlook for 2027
Management expects revenue to increase sequentially in 4Q26 as delayed
customer deliveries resume and inventory begins to normalize. Gross margin

should improve from 3Q26 levels, although management remained cautious given
potential year-end inventory adjustments. The 3Q26 inventory provisions largely
reflected customer-specific products rather than structural obsolescence, and
some provisions could potentially be reversed following successful deliveries.
Nevertheless, management acknowledged that inventory buffers will likely remain
structurally higher than historical levels to accommodate volatile AI infrastructure

deployment schedules. Raw material costs have increased approximately 2% year-
to-date, prompting ongoing pricing negotiations with customers, with limited
recovery expected in 4Q26 and a more meaningful impact in 2027. Operating
expenses will continue increasing in absolute terms to support R&D and capacity
expansion. Management remains positive on 2027 revenue growth, although it did
not provide quantitative guidance. Separately, management expects no near-term

shipment disruption from the ongoing Vicor intellectual property litigation.
We lower earnings projection on power shortages, labor constraints, financing
conditions, and regulatory uncertainties could create quarterly shipment volatility,
but we believe its multiyear AI infrastructure growth cycle intact. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$1,715 (based on lower PER of 35x of 2027 EPS, from 40x previously)

### Page 7

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
7
Delta Electronics
Company description
Delta was established in 1975 and is the world's leading computer switching
power supply manufacturer. Its business is divided into: 1) Power Electronics
(59% of 2022 revenue), including Switching Power Supply, Components,
Display and EV Solution; 2) Infrastructure (27% of revenue), including Wind
power, Telecom power, UPS, EV Infrastructure, Networking; 3) Automation

(14% of revenue), including Industrial Automation (IA), Building Automation;
and 4) Others ( <1% of revenue).
Investment strategy
We rate Delta Electronics shares as Buy due to its strong execution and the
ability to expand margin through favorable product mix shift to AI power and
thermal solutions. We believe EV business slowdown is in the price, and we
expect margins to improve driven by AI solution spec upgrades and expanding
business scale. Delta is now positioning itself as an infrastructurer enabler

rather than a pure component supplier, which deepens its key position in the
AI supply chain.
Valuation
Our target price for Delta Electronics of NT$1,715 is based on 35x 2027E EPS
estimate. Our target multiple is set at the higher end of its trading range since
2023, which we believe is warranted by Delta’s strong earnings growth and
better product mix prospects. We also believe the company’s leading industry
position, long product development cycle, and strong engagement with

customers should translate into high orders/earnings visibility.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
are: 1) lower contribution from non-PC businesses; 2) products in the new
businesses (LED lighting, electric vehicle, and Voice) growing more slowly
than expected; and 3) PC power supply losing market share.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Bull/Bear: Globalwafers Co (6488.TWO)

### Page 6

Globalwafers Co (6488.TWO)
04 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Globalwafers Co
Company description
GWC is the world's third-largest silicon wafer manufacturer. The company
offers a one-stop shop for customers with its wide range of product portfolio
and global presence. GWC has 16 manufacturing sites in 10 countries spread
across Asia, Europe, and the Americas. Its product offering include annealed,
polished, non-polished, diffused, FZ, EPI, SOI wafers ranging from 4” to 12” in

diameter.
Investment strategy
We rate GWC as Buy as we expect its sales and earnings to recover in 2027.
We believe silicon wafer sector has hit the bottom in 2Q26, evidenced by UTR
improvements across 6” to 12” wafers. Although GM would likely face short-
term headwinds in 2H26 due to rising depreciation burden, a fire incident and
higher energy costs, we believe favorable pricing trend, full UTR and the
reception of US government’s subsidies would help offset the negative

impact, especially in 2027.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$920 is based on 28x 2H27-1H28E EPS, in line with
+1stdv of 1-year fwd PE in the past 5 years. We use this PE multiple to reflect a
gradually improving demand outlook for the overall semiconductor industry
from 2H26 onward, especially a more meaningful recovery in 2027 and likely
shortage. We believe the upcoming megatrend demand from AI, HPC, auto,
and Io T and supply tightness could justify GWC's valuations during recovery

backdrop from 1Q27 onwards.
Risks
Key downside risks that could cause the GWC shares to trade below our
target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected pull-in demand; 2)
undisciplined capacity expansion by other industry participants; 3) wafer
price decline driven by macro factors; and 4) late reception of the subsidies
from the US government.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Grand Process Technology Corp. (3131.TWO): SoIC / PLP to support 
future GM enhancement; 3Q26 GM miss
3 November 2026 | 10:54PM HKT
GPTC reported its 3Q26 preliminary results. Revenues was pre-announced, while GM 
came in at 36.6% (down 2.3ppt QoQ; down 4.2ppt YoY), below GSe/cons. of 
39.7%/40.6%, which we attribute to an unfavorable product mix. Opex ratio further 
improved to 14.6% (vs. 17.7% in 2Q26), better than our / Bloomberg consensus 
estimates of 15.1% / 16.3%, leading OP income to +34% QoQ, or +54% YoY. Overall, 
the miss on GM leads EPS miss our / Bloomberg consensus by 9% / 14%. Despite 
3Q26 GM miss, we believe GPTC’s multi-year growth outlook remains intact, with 
continuous capacity increase to better capture the demand from CoWoS capacity 
expansion, along with product mix upgrade toward SoIC and PLP , carrying higher 
content value. Maintain Buy.

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and cut EPS by 4% / 2% / 0% in 
2026-28E, mainly on lower GM, as we slowdown the pace of product mix upgrade 
toward SoIC and PLP . Despite the cut, we continue to expect GM sequential 
enhancement, with 4Q26 GM up to 37.7% (vs. 36.6% in 3Q26) and up to 40.2% / 
42.5% in 2027-28E, driven by rising SoIC and PLP contribution.

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

8028.TT 昇陽半導體 
 2027 年毛利率大幅上修

[公司拜訪]   
   公司簡介 
昇陽半導體(PSI)成立於 1997 年，以可重複使用的晶圓材料-再生晶圓起家，提供晶圓
加工及晶圓薄化兩大事業項目。以技術創新與品質優先的敬業精神，拓展全新的測試
晶圓並開發寬能隙半導體的薄化技術，使客戶享有更具競爭力的全方位服務  
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 284 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，305 
【大盤指數/股價】43308 / 214

➢ 3Q26 毛利率符合預期 
2Q26 營收 14.27 億元(QoQ+9.5%)伴隨著產能開出營收/出貨量都創下
歷史新高，受惠於規模經濟放大影響，2Q26 毛利率 37.89%較前季增加
0.69pp，EPS 1.71 元約略符合預期，3Q26 營收 15.32 億元(QoQ＋
7.36%) ，毛利率 37.2% ，稅後獲利 3.37 億元， QoQ+9.15% ，
YoY+67.01%，EPS 1.84 元。 
➢ 4Q26 營運開始加溫，2026 年財報仍可達標 
以目前市況來看，再生晶圓產能利用率仍滿載，昇陽半 導體為少數近年
來積極配合台積電 (2330)擴產的廠商將直接受惠，但受到機台交期影
響，昇陽半導體 2026 年底產能下修到 110 萬片/月，主要產能開出時間
點在 Q4，昇陽半導體也藉由流程優化提升產出，國票預估 4Q26 營收
為 16.40 億元(QoQ＋7.05%)，毛利率 37.7%，稅後獲利 3.74 億元，
QoQ+11.24%，YoY+66.66%，EPS 2.04 元，由於目前匯率優於預期，
昇陽半導體 2026 年毛利率仍可達到 37.59%的優異水準 (薄化優於預
期)，EPS 僅略微下修到 7.20 元(原估 7.30 元)。 
➢ 被訂單追得跑，中港二廠產能將提前開出 
再生晶圓目前利用率呈現滿載主要受惠於 1)先進製程對再生晶圓不管是
質＆量都增加，HBM 相關 Carrier wafer 需求也在增加，2)台積電未來
月需求大於 200 萬片，且降低自身 in-house 比重。3)台積電海外大幅
擴廠，海外廠升級也帶動再生晶圓較先前進一步上修，昇陽半 導體中港
二廠(預估月產能>60 萬片)將提前於 3Q27 開出，預估中港廠最終產能
可達 120 萬片以上 。 國票預估2026 年/2027 年 EPS 分別為 7.20 元/10.15
元的優異水準，分別年增 64.76% / 40.97%。 
➢ 維持昇陽半導體買進評等，目標價 284 元 
伴隨著台積電大幅海外擴廠以及 2nm 擴充進度 ， 昇陽半導體市佔率將由
2024 年的 38%提升到 2028 年的 45%， 毛利率也伴隨著自動化/規模經
濟而大幅改善 ， 我們認為昇陽半導體為少數受惠於 2nm 製程的個股 ， 投
資價值浮現 ， 近5 年 PE 區間約為 21X~51X(晶圓薄化轉虧為盈 ， 且未來
毛利率將改善)，目前 2026 年 PE 約 26.3X，投資評等維持買進評等，
目標價 284 元(2027F PE 28X)。

個股報告

3Q26 毛利率符合預期 
2Q26 營收 14.27 億元(QoQ+9.5%)伴隨著產能開出營收/出貨量都創下歷史新高，受
惠於規模經濟放大影響，2Q26 毛利率 37.89%較前季增加 0.69pp，EPS 1.71 元約略
符合預期，3Q26 營收 15.32 億元(QoQ＋7.36%)，毛利率 37.2%，稅後獲利 3.37 億
元，QoQ+9.15%，YoY+67.01%，EPS 1.84 元。 
4Q26 營運開始加溫，2026 年財報仍可達標 
以目前市況來看，再生晶圓產能利用率仍滿載，昇陽半 導體為少數近年來積極配合台
積電擴產的廠商將直接受惠，但受到機台交期影響，昇陽半 導體 2026 年底產能下修
到 110 萬片/月，主要產能開出時間點在 Q4，昇陽半導體也藉由流程優化提升產出，
國票預估 4Q26 營收為 16.40 億元(QoQ＋7.05%)，毛利率 37.7%，稅後獲利 3.74 億
元，QoQ+11.24%，YoY+66.66%，EPS 2.04 元，由於目前匯率優於預期，昇陽半導
體 2026 年毛利率仍可達到 37.59%的優異水準(薄化優於預期)，EPS 僅略微下修到 7.20
元(原估 7.30 元)。 
被訂單追得跑，中港二廠產能將提前開出 
再生晶圓目前利用率呈現滿載主要受惠於 1)先進製程對再生晶圓不管是質＆量都增
加，HBM 相關 Carrier wafer 需求也在增加，2)台積電未來月需求大於 200 萬片，且
降低自身 in-house 比重。3)台積電海外大幅擴廠，海外廠升級也帶動再生晶圓較先前
進一步上修，昇陽半導體中港二廠(預估月產能>60 萬片)將提前於 3Q27 開出，預估
中港廠最終產能可達 120 萬片以上。相關同業方面：RS 計畫在 2026 年投資 28 億日
圓、將台灣 12 吋再生晶圓月產能提高至 33 萬片；2027 年投資 124 億日圓，將在台
灣打造第 2 工廠 ， 目標將台灣12 吋再生晶圓月產能提高至 42 萬片(第 1 工廠 37 萬片 、
第 2 工廠 5 萬片)；2028 年將投資 95 億日圓、 將台灣月產能進一步擴增至57 萬片(第
1 工廠 37 萬片、第 2 工廠 20 萬片，RS 計畫在 2026 年~2028 年期間合計投資超過
173 億日圓(分別為 119 億日圓、54 億日圓、未定)擴充日本 12 吋再生晶圓產能，目
標在 2026 年將日本 12 吋再生晶圓月產能提高至 38 萬片(2025 年為 34 萬片)、2027
年提高至 47 萬片，2028 年進一步提高至 52 萬片、在中國的部分，RS 計畫在 2028
年投資 100 億日圓、將中國 12 吋再生晶圓月產能提高至 10 萬片(目前為 5 萬片)。以
目前的投資計畫來看，屆時總產能為 119 萬片，中砂目前月產能為 40 萬片，不過在
記憶體相關市佔率優於昇陽半導體，國票預估 2026 年/2027 年 EPS 分別為 7.20 元
/10.15 元的優異水準，分別年增 64.76% /40.97%。 
預晶圓薄化有利於產品組合 
DrMOS 主要用在 CPU/GPU/ASIC 的 VRM 多相供電。AI 加速器功耗從傳統伺服器等
級一路往 700W、1,000W 以上走，電流越來越大，在用量翻倍方面，傳統通用伺服器
僅需 16 至 32 顆 DrMOS 供電給主晶片與記憶體 ， 而主流8-GPU 架構的 AI 伺服器(如
HGX H100、B200)因單晶片功耗動輒 700W 至 1,000W，供電相數大幅增加，單台用
量一舉攀升至 320 至 400 顆以上 ， 成長逾10 倍 ； 若進入整機櫃架構(如 GB200/GB300 
NVL72) ，整櫃 DrMOS 需求量 更高達 數千顆 。同 時，市 場規格 主流正 從過 往的  
50A–90A 升級至 100A–130A+的高階 SPS，帶動產品單價(ASP)與市場產值雙雙飛
升，目前昇陽半導體的薄化事業部受惠於 DrMOS 的需求增溫，產能利用率大幅度提
升，昇陽半導體當前晶圓薄化月產能約 4 萬片。

維持昇陽半導體買進評等，目標價 284 元

38%提升到 2028 年的 45%，毛利率也伴隨著自動化/規模經濟而大幅改善，我們認為

12%以上

12%

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

### Page 1

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

Document 251: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면  이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다LG에너지솔루션(373220) ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

2026.11.04   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다 Company Brief

3Q26 실적, 원-달러 환율 하락에도 시장 기대치 대폭 상회 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 9.6조원(+59% YoY, + 28% QoQ), 영업이익 7,560억원 
(+26% YoY, +568% QoQ)으로 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 시장기대치(매출 
8.8조원, 영업이익 3,217억원)를 크게 상회했다. 당사는 Stellantis와의 캐나다 
합작관계 종료에 따른 보상금 등을 포함해 약 4,250억원의 일회성 이익이 반영된 
것으로 추정한다. 이를 제외한 영업이익은 약 3,310억원으로, 시장기대치에 대체로 
부합한 것으로 판단된다. AMPC는 미국 ESS 생산량 증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 
힘입어 전분기 대비 약 73% 증가한 4,169억원을 기록했다. 사업부문별로는 ESS의 
실적 개선이 두드러졌다. 상반기 배터리 패키징 병목으로 이연됐던 물량 출하가 
집중되면서 ESS 출하량은 전분기 대비 60% 가량 증가한 것으로 추정된다. 자동차 
전지도 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
33%로 증가했으며, 고정비 부담 완화에 따라 적자폭도 뚜렷하게 축소된 것으로 
판단된다. 소형전지는 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 출하 증가로 매출 
회복과 수익성을 뒷받침한 것으로 보인다. 
4Q26 ESS 중심의 수익성 개선과 수주 모멘텀 지속될 전망 
미국 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 확대와 AI 데이터센터의 전력 수요 증가에 힘입어 
빠르게 성장하고 있다. 특히 정책 환경도 비PFE 공급망을 확보한 업체에 유리하게 
작용하고 있다. 동사는 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스 콜에서 ESS 수주잔고가 약 
160GWh이며, 2028 년까지 캐파의 대부분에 대응하는 물량을 확보했다고 밝혔다. 
또한 현재 다수의 고객사들과 2028년 이후 공급 물량을 협의하고 있어 연말까지 
추가적인 수주 모멘텀이 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이를 바탕으로 4Q26에도 동사의 
본업 실적은 ESS 중심의 개선세를 이어갈 전망이다. 전분기에 반영된 대규모 일회성 
이익의 기저효과로 영업이익은 감소하겠지만, 이를 제외하면 출하량 증가와 가동률 
상승에 따른 점진적인 수익성 개선이 예상된다. 수익성 중심의 사업 효율화를 위해 
소형 IT용 파우치 사업을 축소하고 있다는 점도 중장기 손익 개선에 긍정적이다. 이를 
반영한 4Q26 매출과 영업이익은 각각 약 10.5조원(+63% YoY, +9% QoQ), 
4,150억원(흑자전환 YoY, -45% QoQ)으로 추정된다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 북미 ESS 수요 증가와 공급망 
재편에 따른 신규 수주 확대가 중장기 실적 성장을 뒷받침할 전망이다. 특히 ESS용 
생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화되면 매출 증가에 더해 고정비 부담 완화와 
AMPC 수혜 확대가 맞물리면서 수익성도 개선될 것으로 예상된다. ESS 의 역할이 
신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AI 데이터센터의 안정적인 전력 공급을 
지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 이에 따라 
동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 산업의 
성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다. 업종 내 실적 개선 가시성과 정책 
수혜가 뚜렷하고, 4Q26 신규 수주에 따른 주가 상승 가능성도 높다는 점에서 동사에 
대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다는 판단이다.

 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

### Page 1

纳斯达克 100 收跌超 6% ；科技股整体市值⼀⽇蒸发 2.5 万亿美元；芯⽚指数创疫情以来最⼤
跌幅，收盘闪迪跌 22% 、 AMD 跌 17% 、英伟达跌 9.3% ； AI 基础设施类股 Lumentum 跌近
19% 、 Bloom Energy 跌 16% ；特斯拉跌超 10% 。从芯⽚制造商、电⼒供应商、计算服务
商、软件企业到数据中⼼开发商，⼏乎没有科技业的⻆落幸免周四抛售，科技股积累的⾼
估值和拥挤交易进⼀步放⼤跌幅。与此前资⾦轮动⾄其他板块不同，这次抛售扩散⾄标普
全部⾏业板块。
摘要：
美东时间 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇周四，美股科技股遭遇猛烈抛售， AI 交易⼤撤退，芯⽚、云计算、数据中⼼能
源及光通信等产业链全线重挫。纳指收跌 5.52% ，创 2025 年 4 ⽉美国对等关税宣布以来最⼤跌
幅，纳斯达克 100 指数收跌约 6.4% ，创 2020 年 3 ⽉以来最⼤跌幅。
科技股抛售迅速蔓延⾄整个美股市场。三⼤美股指中，纳指到收盘较 10 ⽉ 6 ⽇所创的纪录⾼位回

撤超过 10% ，正式进⼊技术性调整区间；标普 500 收跌 4.36% ，创 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⼤单⽇跌
幅；；周三⼤涨 3% 的道指虽然开盘涨逾 320 点，但早盘转跌，收跌逾 1830 点、跌幅 3.44% 。
媒体估算，美股整体市值周四蒸发⼀⽇约 2.8 万亿美元，刷新了新冠疫情期间所创的单⽇最⾼纪
录 2.69 亿美元，其中，科技股整体市值周四当天蒸发约 2.5 万亿美元。

### Page 2

这轮抛售并⾮由单⼀因素引发。⺠主党在中期选举中取得重⼤胜利，令市场重新评估美国数据
中⼼建设可能⾯临的政策和监管⻛险；与此同时， AI 巨额资本开⽀的回报周期、融资成本以及数
据中⼼项⽬的实际交付能⼒，正受到越来越多质疑。⾼通业绩及展望未能完全满⾜市场预期，
以及微软对部分新兴云服务商项⽬延期和成本问题的担忧，也令投资者对 AI 产业链的盈利前景更
加谨慎。
AMD 跌 17% ，闪迪重挫 22% ，芯⽚股遭遇疫情以来最惨烈抛售
半导体板块成为本轮抛售的重灾区。美国中期选举结果出炉后，周三已经收跌超过 3% 的费城半
导体指数（ SOX ）周三已收跌超 3% ，周四跌幅进⼀步扩⼤，收跌逾 12% ，创 2020 年 3 ⽉以来、
即新冠疫情初期以来最⼤单⽇跌幅。
个股⽅⾯， AI 芯⽚⻰头英伟达（ NVDA ）收跌约 9.3% ，抹平今年内所有累计涨幅转跌；闪迪
（ SNDK ）收跌约 22.3% ，美光（ MU ）跌约 17.5% ， AMD （ AMD ）和西部数据（ WDC ）跌约

17% ，博通（ AVGO ）和英特尔（ INTC ）跌约 10% ，⾼通（ QCOM ）收跌约 9.5% 。芯⽚股从 AI
算⼒⻰头到存储芯⽚⼚商⼏乎⽆⼀幸免。
芯⽚股集体下挫，既反映了市场对 AI 基础设施投资前景的担忧，也与此前半导体板块估值和资⾦
拥挤程度较⾼有关。 AI 算⼒需求持续增⻓，并不意味着所有芯⽚⼚商都能同步兑现业绩增⻓，投
资者开始更加关注订单兑现、产品结构、利润率以及资本开⽀转化为收⼊的实际进度。
⾼通的业绩为芯⽚板块提供了⼀个直接的负⾯催化剂。该公司最新公布的业绩和展望未能完全
打消市场对智能⼿机业务疲软的担忧，尤其是⼿机芯⽚销售预期不及华尔街预期。路透社指
出，⾼通第四季度智能⼿机芯⽚销售未达市场预期，疲弱需求以及苹果相关收⼊下降是重要拖
累。
虽然⾼通正积极拓展数据中⼼、汽⻋等新市场，但新业务的增⻓尚未充分转化为近期盈利改
善。市场不仅在重新评估⾼通⾃⾝的盈利前景，也在审视 AI 芯⽚⾏业的⾼增⻓预期能否普遍兑
现。

### Page 3

CoreWeave 、 Nebius 等 AI 基础设施企业集体跳⽔
抛售并未⽌步于芯⽚制造商，其他 AI 基础设施相关企业也遭遇⼤幅下跌。
受益于数据中⼼建设热潮的新兴云计算服务商 CoreWeave （ CRWV ）盘中跌超 10% ，收跌
9.8% ， Nebius （ NBIS ）⼀度跌近 20% ，收跌近 15% ；数据中⼼能源供应商 Bloom Energy
（ BE ）盘中跌幅⼀度超过 20% ，收跌 16.4% 。甲⻣⽂（ ORCL ）⼀度跌近 10% ，收跌近 7% 。
光通信板块同样承压， Lumentum （ LITE ）收跌约 18.7% ， Coherent （ COHR ）收跌约 13% 。这
些公司的共同特点是，业务增⻓在不同程度上依赖 AI 数据中⼼持续扩张，因⽽更容易受到投资者
对数据中⼼建设速度和资本回报预期变化的冲击。
微软近期对部分新兴云服务商的态度变化，也令市场对 AI 基础设施产业链的⻛险更加敏感。
彭博社 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇报道称，微软对部分新兴云服务商（ Neocloud ）的合作项⽬出现延误和成本问题

愈发不满，涉及 IREN 、 Nebius 和 Nscale 等企业。相关项⽬的交付进度、基础设施建设和成本控
制问题，凸显了 AI 算⼒需求虽然旺盛，但将规划中的数据中⼼容量按时建成并投⼊运营并⾮易
事。
这对依赖⼤型科技公司订单和融资⽀持的新兴云服务商尤其重要。它们往往需要先投⼊巨额资
⾦建设数据中⼼、采购 GPU 和配套设备，再通过向客⼾出租算⼒逐步回收成本。⼀旦项⽬延
期，便可能同时⾯临收⼊确认推迟、融资成本增加和现⾦流承压等问题。
因此，市场担忧的已经不只是 AI 数据中⼼最终会建多少，⽽是这些项⽬能否按计划交付、能否获
得⾜够的电⼒和设备，以及投⼊的巨额资⾦能否产⽣与估值相匹配的回报。
特斯拉跌超 10% ， Mag 7 市值蒸发约 1 万亿美元
⼤型科技股也未能躲过这轮抛售。

### Page 4

“ 科技七巨头 ” （ Mag 7 ）中，在得州运营 AI 超算基础设施的特斯拉（ TSLA ）收跌约 10.8% ，英伟
达收跌约 9.3% ； Meta （ META ）收跌约 4% ，微软（ MSFT ）收跌 3.6% 亚⻢逊（ AMZN ）和⾕歌
⺟公司 Alphabet （ GOOGL ）分别跌逾 2% 和近 2% ，⾼开低⾛的苹果（ AAPL ）跌幅相对温和，
收跌约 1.5% 。
《华尔街⽇报》统计，科技股整体市值⼀⽇蒸发约 2.5 万亿美元，其中 “ 科技七巨头 ” 就损失约 1 万
亿美元。这轮抛售不仅冲击了此前涨幅巨⼤的 AI 芯⽚和数据中⼼企业，也令⼤型科技公司的估值
承压。
值得注意的是，⼤型科技公司的股价下跌，并不完全意味着市场认为 AI 需求正在消失。更深层的
问题在于， AI 基础设施投资规模持续扩⼤，但投资者对投⼊何时能够转化为利润、现有业务能否
从 AI 中获得⾜够增量收⼊，仍缺乏确定性。
⼤型科技公司不断增加数据中⼼、芯⽚和相关基础设施⽀出，部分企业还通过债券市场融资⽀

持投资计划。在利率仍处于较⾼⽔平的背景下，资本开⽀的规模、融资成本和投资回报周期正
受到更严格的审视。
科技巨头股价下跌并不完全意味着市场认为 AI 需求正在消失。更深层的问题在于， AI 基础设施投
资规模持续扩⼤，但投资者对投⼊何时能够转化为利润、现有业务能否从 AI 中获得⾜够增量收
⼊，仍缺乏确定性。
⼤型科技公司不断增加数据中⼼、芯⽚和相关基础设施⽀出，部分企业还通过债券市场融资⽀
持投资计划。在利率仍处于较⾼⽔平的背景下，资本开⽀的规模、融资成本和投资回报周期正
受到更严格的审视。
⺠主党中期选举⼤胜，数据中⼼建设⾯临政策不确定性
政治因素是这轮 AI 基础设施股票抛售的重要催化剂。
美国中期选举中，⺠主党取得重⼤胜利，市场开始重新评估美国政府对 AI 产业的政策⽀持⼒度，
以及数据中⼼扩张可能⾯临的监管环境变化。
此前，特朗普政府⼀直积极推动 AI 基础设施建设，将数据中⼼、芯⽚和能源供应视为美国保持 AI
竞争优势的重要⽀柱。但随着⺠主党在国会取得优势，投资者担⼼， AI 数据中⼼建设可能⾯临更

多审查，尤其是在⼟地使⽤、能源供应、环保要求和项⽬审批等⽅⾯。
这种担忧并⾮没有现实基础。美国部分地区的居⺠和地⽅政府已经对数据中⼼快速扩张表达反
对，原因包括数据中⼼⼤量消耗电⼒和⽔资源，以及可能推⾼当地居⺠的电费。⺠主党在部分
关键数据中⼼建设州取得胜利，也让市场更加关注地⽅政策可能发⽣的变化。
不过，政策⻛险并不意味着美国 AI 投资必然停⽌。更现实的影响可能是部分项⽬审批周期拉⻓、
建设成本上升，以及企业需要重新评估数据中⼼的选址和投资节奏。
AI 投资回报与融资压⼒再受拷问，资⾦加速撤离拥挤交易
除了中期选举带来的政策不确定性，市场对 AI 投资商业模式和盈利能⼒的担忧，才是这轮科技股
调整背后更深层的因素。
过去⼀段时间， AI 需求爆发推动⼤型科技公司持续上调资本开⽀，芯⽚、云计算、数据中⼼、电
⼒设备和光通信企业也因此享受估值溢价。但随着投资规模不断扩⼤，市场开始从单纯追逐 AI 需
求增⻓，转向审视巨额投⼊究竟能产⽣多少实际收⼊和利润。

4 条评论
《华尔街⽇报》指出，此次抛售波及亚洲芯⽚制造商、美国电⼒供应商、计算服务商、软件企
业以及数据中⼼开发商，⼏乎没有科技产业链的⻆落能够幸免。与此前资⾦从 AI 股票轮动⾄其他
板块不同，这⼀次抛售扩散⾄标普 500 指数全部 11 个⾏业板块，显⽰市场⻛险偏好出现更⼴泛的
收缩。
与此同时， AI 企业对债务融资的依赖、部分⼤型科技公司财报表现未能满⾜市场期待，也加剧了
投资者的谨慎情绪。市场不仅担忧 AI 基础设施投资规模过⼤，还开始质疑这些投资能否在合理时
间内产⽣⾜够回报。
英国《⾦融时报》报道称，部分对冲基⾦正在迅速削减仓位，资⾦转向现⾦，市场中愿意逢低
买⼊的投资者明显减少。随着抛售持续扩⼤，科技股此前积累的⾼估值和拥挤交易进⼀步放⼤

了跌幅。
此 外 ，新 兴 云 服 务 商 ⾯ 临 的 压 ⼒ 尤 其 明 显 。 与 资 ⾦ 实 ⼒ 雄 厚 的 超 ⼤ 规 模 云 服 务 商 相 ⽐ ，
Neocloud 企业往往更加依赖外部融资和少数⼤型客⼾。⼀旦数据中⼼项⽬延期，或客⼾推迟接
收算⼒容量，企业就可能⾯临前期投⼊⽆法及时转化为收⼊的问题。
从市场表现看，这不是⼀次单纯针对某家公司的业绩利空，⽽是此前⾼度集中的 AI 交易⾯临多重
因素冲击：政策⻛险上升、资本开⽀回报受到质疑、部分基础设施项⽬出现延期，以及个别芯
⽚公司的业绩指引不及预期，共同推动资⾦撤离科技股。
周三，中期选举结果明朗曾推动美股⼤幅反弹，标普 500 指数创下三个⽉来最⼤单⽇涨幅；但仅
仅⼀个交易⽇后，市场便重新聚焦 AI 产业链的政策、融资和盈利⻛险。此次历史性抛售不仅抹去
了前⼀⽇的选举后反弹，也表明投资者对 AI 交易的态度正在发⽣变化：从追逐算⼒扩张，转向更
加严格地审视投资回报、项⽬执⾏和估值。

东吴证券研究所

A股10月复盘与11月展望——全球AI信
用重定价下的调整，与政策维稳下的磨底期

2026年11月04日

证券分析师  芦哲 
执业证书：S0600524110003 
 luzhe@dwzq.com.cn 
证券分析师  唐遥衎 
执业证书：S0600524120016 
 tangyk@dwzq.com.cn

2026-11-04

2026-11-03

[Table_Tag]    [Table_Summary] 投资要点 
◼ 10月行情的本质：AI信用重定价与内部去拥挤共振： 
◼ 2026年10月万得全A回调12.44%，为2016年1月以来第三大单月跌
幅，呈“上旬下行、中旬弱势、下旬加速”三段式，收于月内低点。与
9月不同，10月美债10年期收益率下行约35.6个基点至4.929%，A股
却加速回调，主导矛盾已从“无风险利率”切换为“AI叙事信用审视+
高拥挤、高杠杆筹码被动去化”。全月日均成交16097亿元、缩量12.1%，
两融余额降1833亿元，属存量资金被动降杠杆，而非恐慌出逃。 
◼ 行业分化：防御与能源相对占优，AI链全面重定价： 
◼ 2026年10月行业分化程度较高，31个申万一级行业中仅5个正收益，
且全部集中于“防御+能源”：食品饮料+5.31%、银行+4.84%、石油石
化+3.34%、煤炭+1.29%、家电+0.56%；红利指数+2.33%。领跌为通信-
30.23%、电子-25.70%、建材-17.96%、综合-17.31%、电力设备-15.88%。
电子1—9月利润同比增1.0倍而股价单月回调25.70%，“业绩向上、
股价向下”表明10月调整可能更多反映估值重估与持仓结构变化，而
非仅由当期盈利表现解释。 
◼ 宏观背景：外部AI信用冲击，内部政策与数据拉锯： 
◼ 2026年10月，海外AI定价从“资本开支扩张=增长”切换为“回报与
信用风险审视”：英特尔单日回调28.58%，亚马逊800亿美元供应商贷
款引发融资结构担忧，存储芯片指数全月回调约24.8%，费城半导体回
调超过15%，纳指回调5.30%，标普500仅回调0.93%。通胀黏性压缩
转松空间：美国9月CPI同比3.7%、PCE同比3.6%，布伦特原油涨
13.6%；10月FOMC维持3.75%—4.00%不变但两名票委主张加息。国
内总量平稳、结构分化，10月制造业PMI回落至49.3%；五中全会后
金融管理部门密集释放“稳市场”信号。 
◼ 市场核心分歧解析： 
◼ 核心分歧集中于六问：10月市场回调更宜理解为“AI信用重定价+内部
筹码去化”的叠加影响；美债收益率回落与A股调整同期出现，说明单
一利率因素对当月走势的解释力有限。政策维稳有助于稳定预期、降低
极端波动风险，后续企稳仍需政策支持、基本面预期与交易结构改善共
同验证，短期或震荡修复，中期仍待信号确认，快速V型反转偏乐观情
形。双创风险度处于历史低位，赔率有所提升；但回调幅度本身并非反
弹持续性的充分条件，胜率提升还需“AI信用冲击缓和+成交承接改善”
双重确认。防御红利空间有限，同时需关注交易拥挤度上升后的性价比
变化。微盘、北证去拥挤在量能层面已较充分，趋势层面仍需跟踪确认。
11月日历效应可作为有限但可参考的季节性线索。 
◼ 11月展望： 
◼ 2026年11月权益综合信号虽转为-2，但万得全A风险度处于2.7%较低
分位，短期下行空间受限、超跌修复概率上升；基准情景由‘震荡磨底’
调整为‘短期震荡修复、中期磨底确认’。历史-2样本偏谨慎，但当前
低风险度与政策维稳密集，短期不宜简单套用历史均值。 
◼ 核心结论与总结 
◼ 2026年10月核心是“全球AI信用重定价”与“内部拥挤端去化”共振
深调，而非国内基本面恶化或海外利率上行；月末政策维稳信号密集，
但指数收于月内低点，政策底信号与市场底筹码仍有时滞。11月是“政
策维稳+超跌约束”与“外部风险窗口+AI信用冲击”的拉锯，基准为短
期震荡修复，中期持续性看‘成交回升+AI链企稳’双确认。结构上短期
超跌成长弹性更大，防御/能源可作底仓；微盘与北证在增量工具落地后
弹性有望改善。 
◼ 风险提示：美国中期选举后地缘升级；美联储12月加息；AI信用冲击
延续；去杠杆负反馈；政策维稳不及预期；数据口径风险。整体需关注
海外利率、地缘冲突、微盘拥挤与政策兑现

1.   2026年10月行情复盘：一场"不是利率"的调整 
1.1.   传导闭环：为何利率回落未能带动A股同步修复 
 2026年10月A股的回调，拆开看是一条与9月方向相反的四步传导链： 
【叙事端】全球AI信用重定价（英特尔盈利指引不及预期、存储链回调、科技巨
头资本开支与现金流压力） → 【结构端】A股AI硬件链（电子/通信）深度回调→双
创指数失去核心支撑 → 【资金端】高融资余额下的被动降杠杆（两融余额月内大幅回
落、成交缩量）→ 拥挤端（双创/微盘/北证）集中去化 → 【风格端】仅存防御与能源
正收益，市场风险偏好回落，正收益行业范围收窄。 
 这条链的每一环都有数据支撑： 
【叙事端】：2026年10月全球市场对AI的定价逻辑从"资本开支扩张=增长"切换
到"资本开支扩张=回报与信用风险"。标志性事件包括：2026年10月30日英特尔股价
单日回调28.58%（第四季度盈利指引不及市场预期）、亚马逊披露800亿美元供应商贷
款额度引发"AI融资结构"担忧；10月28日SK海力士公布三季度营业利润同比暴增7
倍并启动大规模股东回报后，股价当日上涨10%，但存储链整体在"涨价兑现"与"需求透
支"之间剧烈摆动（存储芯片指数全月回调约24.8%）。"业绩好"与"股价回调"同时出现，
是本轮AI重定价的核心特征——市场开始为"AI资本开支的融资成本与投资回报"定价，
而非为"景气度"定价。 
【结构端】：A股AI硬件链（通信-30.23%、电子-25.70%）的回调幅度远大于其基
本面变化（2026年1-9月电子行业利润同比增长1.0倍，拉动全部规上工业企业利润增
长9.6个百分点），说明10月下的主导变量是估值与筹码，而非业绩。 
【资金端】：全月日均成交16097亿元、环比缩量12.1%；两市融资余额由9月末
25324亿元降至10月29日23492亿元（-1833亿元）——这是2026年以来两融余额幅
度最大的一段回撤，融资盘的被动减持与双创/微盘的回调形成负反馈。 
【风格端】：申万一级行业中仅食品饮料（+5.31%）、银行（+4.84%）、石油石化
（+3.34%）、煤炭（+1.29%）、家用电器（+0.56%）录得正收益，红利指数上涨2.33%、
风险度位于自成立以来66.3%分位；而双创（创业板指-23.02%/科创50-24.13%）、微盘
（-14.74%）、北证50（-14.80%）等拥挤端全面去化。 
根源是"定价锚"的切换：9月A股的下跌是"贴现率上行带来的估值压力"（美债逼
近5.4%），10月美债回落但A股加速下跌，说明当前市场的主导矛盾已从"无风险利率
"切换为"AI叙事的信用与回报审视+内部筹码结构"。隐含的关键推论是：用"美债利率
阈值"（如跌破5.0%）作为A股修复条件在10月被证伪，11月需要观察的是AI信用冲
击的减弱信号（费半企稳、半导体信用利差收敛）与内部杠杆去化的进程（两融余额止
跌）。

### Page 5

5 / 23 
2026年10月深调源于"AI信用重定价+内部去拥挤"的共振，而非国内基本面或海
外利率；这意味着修复路径也不同于前两轮——先等信用冲击减弱与杠杆去化完成，再
谈基本面与政策的重新定价。

1.2.   三段式走势：全月下行，月末收于月内低点 
2026年10月万得全A的下跌呈清晰的"三段式"结构（数据截至2026年10月30
日）：

表1：2026年10月万得全A三段式走势（数据区间2026-09-30至2026-10-30） 
阶段 区间 涨跌 特征 
上旬下行 2026年10月8日-10月14日 -4.41% 节后调整，AI相关板块开始走弱 
中旬弱势 2026年 10月 15日-10月 23日 -2.71% 油价通胀与中选不确定性压制， 10 月 22 日单日 -2.45% 
下旬加速 2026年 10月 26日-10月 30日 -5.85% 10月 27日-2.23%、10月 29日-2.13%、10 月 30 日-2.34%，收于月内低点 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所 
三段式之中，单日较大回调主要出现在以下四个交易日：2026年 10月 22日（-
2.45%）、10月30日（-2.34%）、10月15日（-2.28%）、10月27日（-2.23%）；反
弹仅两个交易日相对显著：10月23日（+2.02%）与10月28日（+1.55%）。月内最高
收盘6107.00点（10月9日）、最低收盘5425.00点（10月30日），全月收于月内最
低、且当日未见明显的护盘承接。 
这一走势结构提示两个关键信息：其一，10月的回调不是"单日恐慌宣泄"（不同于
9月月末的两根大阴线），而是全月三个阶段连续走弱，外生催化（AI信用、油价、中
选）与内生结构（高融资、高拥挤）持续共振，属于典型的"趋势性去化"而非"情绪性错
杀"；其二，月末创月内新低意味着下跌的动能尚未自然衰竭，11月上旬的修复更可能
来自"外力"（政策维稳、外围风险释放）而非"自发的超跌反抽"。

1.3.   南北同跌下的"分化残留"：主要宽基指数涨跌与风险度

表2：主要宽基指数涨跌与风险度（涨跌区间 2026-09-30至2026-10-30，风险度截至 2026-
10-30） 
指数 2026年10月涨跌 风险度（2026-10-30） 风险度历史分位 
万得微盘股指数 -14.74% 73.89 48.9% 
国证 2000 -15.30% 6.45 10.0% 
中证 1000 -15.59% 0.13 3.1% 
中证 500 -15.72% -3.81 1.1% 
深证成指 -15.94% -5.17 2.9% 
创业板指 -23.02% -6.06 1.5% 
科创 50 -24.13% -7.42 1.8% 
上证指数 -6.33% 7.84 14.0% 
上证 50 -2.88% 8.40 14.6% 
沪深 300 -8.54% -0.95 4.1%

红利指数 +2.33% 74.29 66.3% 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所 
备注：风险度TR为拥挤度趋势模型因子口径，按各指数自成立以来测算历史分位；常态
区间0-100、极端行情可超越边界。 
三点观察：其一，"越大越抗跌"取代了9月的"越小越抗跌"——上证 50（-2.88%）
与红利（+2.33%）成为仅有的低波品种，而2026年9月表现最强的微盘、双创、北证
50在10月成为去化的主战场，大小盘剪刀差在10月完成了方向反转；其二，风险度已
大面积落入历史低位——万得全A风险度-1.17（2.7%分位）、创业板指风险度-6.06（1.5%
分位）、科创50风险度-7.42（1.8%分位），大盘成长类资产的"超跌程度"超过2024年
初与2022年10月的水平；其三，防御端拥挤度已不低——红利指数风险度74.29（66.3%
分位）、微盘股风险度自9月末的106.1回落至73.9（48.9%分位），防御方向的"低位
安全垫"正在变薄，而拥挤端经过一个月的去化已回到历史中位附近——这为11月的结
构判断提供了重要前提。

2.   行业分化：防御与能源相对占优，AI链全面重定价 
2026年10月行业表现的分化程度明显：31个申万一级行业中仅5个录得正收益，
且全部集中于"防御+能源"；跌幅前三的通信、电子、建筑材料均超过17%，AI硬件链
（通信/电子）与内需周期链（建材/机械/地产）形成了两个主要承压方向。

排名 行业 2026年 10月涨跌 归因 
1 食品饮料 +5.31% 高股息+低估值防御属性，油价通胀链条下的成本传导相对可控 
2 银行 +4.84% 类债属性+三季报息差企稳，农业银行三季度净利润同比+10.84% 
3 石油石化 +3.34% 布伦特原油单月+13.6%，地缘风险溢价与炼化景气共振 
4 煤炭 +1.29% 能源比价效应与迎峰度冬预期 
5 家用电器 +0.56% 出口韧性+以旧换新政策延续 
6 交通运输 -0.87% 高股息防御属性，跌幅显著小于全市场 
7 公用事业 -2.48% 类债属性与电价支撑，相对抗跌 
8 非银金融 -5.22% 成交收缩与融资盘去化压制，但跌幅仍小于全市场 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所

1 通信 -30.23% AI硬件链重定价，光模块/服务器链系统性回撤 
2 电子 -25.70% 
半导体与存储链下跌（存储芯片指数全
月约-24.8%），估值调整与筹码调整叠
加 
3 建筑材料 -17.96% 地产链疲弱延续，投资端数据持续低迷 
4 综合 -17.31% 小市值与主题资金退潮 
5 电力设备 -15.88% 新能源权重股补跌，与创业板指的深度调整同源 
6 机械设备 -15.04% 中游制造补跌，出口链与内需预期的双重压制 
7 房地产 -15.01% 政策预期退潮，前三季度开发投资同比降20.6% 
8 有色金属 -14.62% 工业金属受全球需求预期压制（与能源链分化） 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所 
归因上有一个容易被忽略的背离：2026年1-9月电子行业利润同比增长1.0倍（国
家统计局口径，拉动全部规上工业企业利润增长9.6个百分点），而电子板块股价单月
下跌25.70%——"业绩向上、股价向下"的背离，表明10月调整可能更多反映估值重估
与持仓结构变化，而非仅由当期盈利表现解释。这一背离对11月的含义是双面的：一
方面，跌深之后的"业绩确定性"标的具备修复弹性基础；另一方面，只要AI叙事层面的

### Page 8

8 / 23 
信用冲击（资本开支融资、回报审视）未减弱，业绩兑现就难以单独支撑股价——修复
需要"叙事企稳"与"业绩兑现"两者同向。 
三季报的另一面：银行（农业银行单季净利润同比+10.84%、六大行前三季合计净
赚约1.14万亿元）、有色金属（矿企与算力金属企业业绩亮眼）、部分高端制造（源杰
科技前三季归母净利润同比+866.88%、中际旭创+211.81%）的业绩兑现度较高，但股价
表现出现分层——"业绩兑现但筹码拥挤"的品种在去杠杆阶段被优先减持，这是 10月
与以往财报季最大的不同。 
3.   宏观背景：外部"AI信用冲击"，内部"政策与数据的拉锯" 
3.1.   海外：AI叙事重定价、通胀黏性与中选变数 
10月最大的海外变化是市场对AI产业前景的定价逻辑发生变化。 2026年10月
中下旬起，美股对AI的定价从"资本开支扩张→景气上行"转向"资本开支扩张→融资成
本与回报审视"：2026年10月30日英特尔股价单日下跌28.58%（四季度指引不及预
期）、高通下跌11.12%、AMD下跌10.88%；亚马逊因披露800亿美元供应商贷款额度
引发"AI债务融资结构"担忧（此前还出现英特尔、亚马逊信用违约互换报价跳升的报道，
规模级别约123.5个基点）；存储链先涨后跌，存储芯片指数全月下跌约24.8%。这轮
重定价的直接后果是美股涨跌结构剧烈分化：标普500单月仅下跌0.93%，但纳指下跌
5.30%、费城半导体指数下跌逾15%，而标普500等权重指数上涨4.94%——"部分指数
调整相对有限、板块表现分化"是10月全球权益市场的共同特征。 
通胀黏性叠加地缘溢价，压缩了货币政策转松的空间。 2026年10月14日公布的
美国9月CPI同比上涨3.7%（高于预期3.6%、前值3.4%）、核心CPI同比2.6%（高
于预期）；10月30日公布的美国9月PCE同比3.6%（5月以来最高）、核心PCE同
比升至3.1%；同期布伦特原油单月上涨13.6%（收于117.64美元/桶、地缘风险溢价主
导）。油价→通胀→利率预期的链条使"美联储转松预期"进一步后延：10月28日FOMC
（美东时间）维持联邦基金利率目标区间3.75%-4.00%不变，库克与哈马克两名票委主
张加息25个基点，会后声明未对12月是否加息给出明确信号；达拉斯联储主席Logan
（2026年票委）会后表示"仍预计至少需要再加息两次、但可循序渐进"。"暂停但未转
向"是本次会议的核心信息。 
美国中期选举与地缘风险构成11月的最大不确定性组合。 美国中期选举将于2026
年11月3日投票，选前民调与库克政治报告均显示民主党形势改善；据多方报道，美
方已通报海湾盟友准备在选举后（最快11月4日）恢复对伊朗的"短时高强度打击"，卡
塔尔等调解方在10月30日-31日于德黑兰、多哈进行密集斡旋（报道口径）。"选举落
地前的观望+选举后的地缘窗口"是 11月海外风险的两段式结构，对应油价的风险溢价
路径：选举前以降温交易为主（10月下旬油价曾单日回落逾2%）、选举后若打击落地
则溢价再度上冲。

3.2.   国内：数据"总量平稳、结构分化"，政策密集维稳 
三季度经济：总量平稳、投资弱、出口强。 2026年10月19日国家统计局公布三
季度数据：前三季度GDP同比增长4.6%（总量107.11万亿元），三季度单季增长4.5%、
环比回升0.2个百分点；9月规模以上工业增加值同比+5.5%（工业机器人、集成电路产
量增长均超18%）、社会消费品零售总额同比+1.1%（剔除汽车拖累后+3.9%）；但1-9
月城镇固定资产投资同比下降7.2%、地产开发投资同比下降20.6%（新建商品房销售额
同比-13.9%），投资端是总量数据中最弱的一环。物价方面：9月CPI同比+0.9%（核心
CPI+1.0%）、PPI同比+5.0%（2022年6月以来最高，其中汽油价格同比+16.6%）——
PPI高读数由能源与输入性因素主导，"再通胀的交易属性"强于"需求属性"。9月城镇调
查失业率5.2%、较上月回落0.1个百分点。 
金融与利润：直接融资占比抬升、工业利润保持两位数增长。 2026年10月14日
央行公布前三季度金融数据：社会融资规模增量累计27.46万亿元（同比少 2.63万亿
元）、9月末社融存量同比+7.1%、M2同比+7.2%、前三季度人民币贷款增加11.52万亿
元；结构上债券与股票融资在社融增量中占比约49.5%、超过贷款占比约7个百分点。

2026年 10月 27日公布的工业利润数据显示：1-9月规模以上工业企业利润同比增长
15.5%（利润总额60337.4亿元）、9月单月同比+14.4%（今年6月以来最高），其中电
子行业利润同比增长1.0倍、高技术制造业利润增长52.0%。"金融数据总量收敛、结构
向直接融资倾斜"与"工业利润由AI链与能源链拉动"并存，是10月基本面数据的主线。 
政策端：从"金融街论坛"到"五中全会"的密集维稳信号。 政策线索按时间排序：
①2026年10月19日-21日金融街论坛年会召开，央行行长潘功胜表示将推动退出存贷
款基准利率、完善"DR001+贷款市场报价利率"的双定价基准体系；②10月26日-29日
党的二十届五中全会在北京召开（研究持之以恒推进全面从严治党若干重大问题、审议
通过《决定》，10月30日举行新闻发布会解读）；③10月30日央行、证监会、金融
监管总局等金融管理部门分别召开会议传达全会精神，其中证监会明确"全力稳市场稳
预期，深化投融资综合改革，完善全链条监管体系，持续提升资本市场韧性和信心"；
④10月30日央行公告公开市场操作"换锚"：自11月2日起每个工作日开展隔夜逆回
购操作招标（操作利率1.25%），7天期逆回购调整为固定数量、利率招标、多重价位
中标并主要在税期、月末等时点开展；⑤全国人大常委会第二十五次会议将于11月2
日-6日举行，金融法草案将提请二次审议（拟加强对资产管理产品与服务的统一规范、
跨境金融活动监管）。"稳市场"表述与操作层面的"换锚"是10月末政策端最重要的边际
变化，其含义是短端利率调控框架向价格型进一步转型、并为跨月流动性提供新的工具
安排。 
10月31日公布的中国10月PMI回落至收缩区间： 制造业PMI 49.3%（前值50.1%）、
非制造业商务活动指数49.7%、综合PMI产出指数与两项分项同向回落——PMI重回荣
枯线下方，与9月数据的"温和改善"形成反差，也为11月政策加码提供了依据。

4.   市场核心分歧解析 
分歧一：这轮下跌的主因是"海外利率"还是"AI叙事+内部结构"？ 市场在 10月
仍习惯用"美债利率"解释A股，但数据不支持：10月美债10年期收益率下行了约35.6
个基点至4.929%，A股却加速下跌。我们的回答是后者：下跌的主因是"AI叙事的信用
重定价"与"高拥挤、高杠杆筹码的被动去化"，海外利率10月反而是"逆风减弱"的。这
一判断的可检验推论是：若11月A股企稳，应首先看到费半企稳、两融余额止跌与AI
链缩量收敛， 而非美债进一步下行——用利率阈值跟踪A股修复条件在10月已被证伪。 
分歧二：政策维稳信号能否让市场"见底"？ 10月末维稳信号密度为2026年最高
（五中全会+证监会“全力稳市场稳预期”+央行换锚+金融法二审），政策信号主要作用
于预期与流动性，而“政策底”向“市场底”的过渡通常还需要筹码出清（融资盘、拥挤端）
与外部风险释放（中选、地缘）形成共振。10月30日指数收于月内低点，量价特征显
示筹码出清仍在推进，完成度需后续数据进一步验证。基准判断是：政策维稳有助于降
低尾部风险、稳定预期与流动性环境；趋势扭转则需要更多条件配合。见底形态更可能
经历缩量整固和反复确认，V型反转可作为乐观情形之一。 
分歧三：超跌成长（双创）能否凭借"跌得多"反弹？ 创业板指风险度分位已降至
1.5%（自成立以来）、科创50风险度分位降至1.8%，“超跌”程度接近或超过历史较低
分位，赔率有所提升。但10月的一个重要证据是：跌幅本身不构成反弹持续性的充分

### Page 12

12 / 23 
条件——本轮回调的传导源（AI信用冲击）仍在全球层面消化（英特尔、亚马逊等事
件），筹码层面（融资盘、主题基金、部分量化产品）的出清进度也需要从价格之外的
多维度确认。10月下旬以来双创曾出现多次单日反抽（10月23日、10月28日），显
示一定弹性，但连续性仍需成交与信用信号配合。判断：双创具备“赔率”，而“胜率”的
确认需要两个条件——AI信用冲击减弱（费半止跌+相关信用指标收敛）与成交结构改
善（缩量止跌或放量承接）；两者出现前，反弹持续性宜按逐步验证处理，关注技术性
反抽向趋势修复转化的条件。 
分歧四：防御与红利还能不能追？ 10月红利指数上涨2.33%、银行上涨4.84%，
防御与能源是当月主要的正收益方向；但防御的拥挤度已经抬升（红利指数风险度74.29、
处自成立以来66.3%分位），且银行业具有"高位放量滞涨"的微观特征（工商银行月内
创历史新高后于月末走弱）。我们的回答：防御依然是基准情景下的相对占优方向，但
其"相对收益属性"强于"绝对收益空间"——在油价与利率的再通胀组合下，防御的性价
比依赖"风险偏好继续收缩"这一前提，该前提若被政策维稳与中选落地削弱，防御的进
攻性会快速衰减。 
分歧五：微盘/北证/双创的"去拥挤"结束了吗？ 10月微盘股风险度由 106.1回落
至73.9（自历史98.2%分位回落至48.9%分位）、短期动量降至9.1，北证50月内回调
14.80%、收于月内低点。拥挤度指标已明显回落，调整进程进入“后半程”，但结束与否
仍需观察；两市融资余额10月下降1833亿元，月末企稳信号仍需跟踪。判断：去拥挤
已进入后半程，短期继续大幅去化的空间有限；若全市场风险偏好修复与政策工具落地，
微盘/北证/双创有望出现阶段性反弹，中期弹性看增量资金。 
分歧六：11月的"日历效应"这次能否相信？ 历史上11月是A股偏强的月份（2000
年以来26次中18次上涨、均值+1.83%），"大幅回调后的次月"的统计（56.1%胜率）
同样偏正面；但2026年以来日历效应已多次失效（3月、7月、9月均未遵循季节性）。
我们的回答：日历效应只提供"弱概率支撑"，不构成方向判断；11月的方向由"政策维稳
的落地强度"与"AI信用冲击的减弱速度"共同决定。

5.   2026年11月展望 
5.1.   量化模型：权益综合信号转为-2 
"权益综合信号"月频量化择时模型以基本面、资金面、国际面、估值面、技术面五
维度打分合成综合信号（正看多、负看空、0中性），据以判断万得全A月度方向，样
本自2016年1月起、数据截至2026年10月31日。

级下调所致，技术面（-1）反映趋势与动量仍待修复，资金面（+1）是唯一正值（政策
维稳与流动性工具"换锚"）。历史上-2信号14次样本中，正收益概率28.57%、平均收
益-2.09%——在所列历史样本中，-2信号对应的平均收益低于-1信号（-1的平均收益为
-1.06%），需要以谨慎为基调；但需要注意，当前万得全A风险度（-1.17、2.7%分位）
所对应的"低位约束"也是历史少见，模型信号与结构数据的组合指向"下行空间受限、但
趋势反转未至"——即：负收益的概率高、但"负收益的幅度"未必重复历史均值，短期更
应理解为“下行空间受限、超跌修复概率上升”；中期趋势反转仍需成交与AI信用信
号确认。负收益概率虽高，但短期修复窗口正在打开。

### Page 14

14 / 23 
5.2.   宽基方向：五维框架 
五个维度并非平行，其中 F2（政策+海外）主导方向，F1 看下行空间，F3 看上行
斜率，F4、F5 分别提供概率参考与最终情景加权。落到2026年11月： 
F1 风险收益结构：整体低位、防御端已不再便宜 
图1：万得全 A指数风险趋势模型（2026/10/30）

数据来源：Wind，东吴证券研究所

2026年10月30日，万得全A风险度-1.17（自成立以来2.7%分位），为2000年
以来历史第11低位区域、低于2016年以来任何一次月末水平（2024年9月20日低点
为-0.55、2022年10月为-0.46）。历史上万得全A风险度≤0的交易日共223天，近五
次典型出现时点及此后表现：2024年9月20日（未来1个月+28.4%、3个月+32.2%）、
2023年6月12日（+0.4%、-1.2%）、2022年10月17日（+0.7%、+3.1%）、2018年
7月9日（-5.1%、-6.9%）、2016年2月15日（+1.6%、+3.5%）。"低风险度"是赔率信
号而非胜率信号：五次中1个月后4次为正但分化较大（+28.4%至-5.1%），3个月后3
次为正——低位意味着"向下的空间有限、向上的路径依赖催化"，与正文分歧二的"政策
底≠市场底"相互印证。 
结构上分两端：超跌端（创业板指-6.06、科创50-7.42、中证500-3.81，分位均处自
成立以来1%-2%较低区间）具备厚安全垫；防御端（红利指数74.29、66.3%分位；银行

### Page 15

15 / 23 
股月内创新高）拥挤度已抬升，"便宜"的成分下降。 
结论：整体风险度较低构成11月"下行空间受限"的核心依据，但方向性机会需要
超跌端出现"催化触发"，防御端的相对优势依赖风险偏好继续收缩。 
F2 主导逻辑边际变化：政策维稳"密度上升" vs 外部风险"两段式" 
强化项（国内）：五中全会（10月26日-29日）后金融管理部门密集表态，证监会
"全力稳市场稳预期"；央行公开市场"换锚"（11月2日起隔夜逆回购常态化、7天期逆
回购调整为利率招标）改善短端利率调控的灵活性；全国人大常委会（11月2日-6日）
审议金融法草案二审稿；政策工具箱在"稳市场"方向的可用性上升。 
加重项（海外，选举前）：美国中期选举前的观望效应（资金降低风险承担），美
股AI链的信用冲击（英特尔、亚马逊事件）仍待减弱，费城半导体指数10月下跌逾15%
后尚未出现趋势性企稳。 
加重项（海外，选举后）：美方被报道筹备选举后恢复对伊朗打击（最快11月 4
日）、卡塔尔斡旋结果待定；12月FOMC（12月8日-9日）加息悬念未解（10月FOMC
两名票委主张加息、Logan称至少还需两次加息）。 
缓和项：美债10年期收益率已回落至4.929%（"利率逆风"边际减弱）；油价在斡
旋进展下出现过单日逾2%的回落（若选举后局势降温，或触发通胀-利率预期的连锁缓
和）；美元在月内冲高后回落。 
结论：11月短期更可能呈现政策维稳与超跌修复主导的震荡上行/修复格局，中期
取决于中选后地缘路径。外部风险两段式意味着短期修复可期、中期波动仍在。11月第
一周的美国中期选举与随后的地缘窗口是本轮宏观定价的"分水岭事件"。 
F3 资金博弈结构：缩量去杠杆的后半程 
2026年10月全市场日均成交16097亿元、较9月的18315亿元缩量12.1%，月末
五个交易日成交在14264亿-19729亿元区间波动、无持续放量迹象；两市融资余额由9
月末的25324亿元降至10月29日的23492亿元（-1833亿元），降幅为2026年以来最
大，且月末仍未出现明确的止跌形态。另一方面，流动性总量维持宽松：央行10月30
日当周公开市场净投放14110亿元（创三个半月单周新高），叠加11月2日起隔夜逆
回购常态化操作的"换锚"，资金利率的波动率预计降低。 
结论：11月资金面处于杠杆去化后半程，短期增量资金系统性入场条件尚不充分，
但政策工具与流动性‘换锚’有望带来边际回流；行情短期以结构修复为主，中期若两融
止跌、成交回升，修复范围有望扩散。 
F4 日历效应与均值回归 
基于万得全A指数（881001.WI）日频原始数据（2000-01至2026-10，共321个月）

### Page 16

16 / 23 
实测： 
表10：大幅回调月后次月日历效应统计（样本区间 2000-01至2026-10） 
统计 样本 结果 
单月回调≤-5%后次月 67次（含 2026年 10月），可统计66次 上涨 37次、胜率 56.1%；次月均值+0.30%、中位+1.08% 
其中：2020年以来 13次（含 2026年 10月），可统计12次 上涨 8次、胜率 66.7%；次月均值+1.12% 
历次 11月（季节性） 26次 上涨18次、胜率69%；均值+1.83%、中位+2.44% 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所 
结论：2026年10月万得全A回调12.44%，达到模型设定的大幅回调阈值；历史
规律显示"大幅回调后的次月偏反弹"（全样本胜率56.1%、2020年以来66.7%），11
月本身也是历史偏强月份（69%胜率）。但需警惕两点：其一，"大幅回调后的次月"的
均值仅+0.30%（中位数+1.08%），且样本中不乏继续回调的案例（2022年3月-7.5%
后次月-9.5%、2022年9月-7.6%后次月-2.5%）；其二，2026年以来日历效应已多次失
效（2026年3月-8.7%后次月+8.7%兑现反弹， 但2026年7月-13.1%后的8月仅+5.4%、
9月-5.3%后的10月更是-12.4%）。综合看，日历效应对11月构成"弱概率支撑"，可
作为"下行空间受限"的辅助证据。

5.4.   行业观察（产业链基本面五条线索） 
线索一：能源与通胀链（石油石化+煤炭+农业）——布伦特原油10月上涨13.6%
（地缘溢价主导），石油石化板块+3.34%、煤炭+1.29%为仅有的两个正收益周期方向。
11月的核心变量是"中选后地缘路径"：若打击落地，油价与炼化景气的交易延续；若斡
旋达成降温，需防溢价回吐。该线索是当前"基本面与事件驱动共振度"最高的方向。 
线索二：防御/红利链（银行+食品饮料+公用事业+交通运输）——类债属性在"缩量
+去杠杆"环境中占优，但拥挤度抬升（红利指数66.3%分位）与"高位滞涨"微观特征提
示：防御的方向价值仍在、上行性价比下降。三季报验证：银行净利润增速企稳（六大
行前三季合计净赚约1.14万亿元）、食品饮料龙头业绩平稳。 
线索三：超跌成长链（电子+通信+计算机）——电子行业三季报净利润同比增长1.0
倍、通信设备（光模块链）业绩兑现度高（中际旭创前三季归母净利润同比+211.81%），
"业绩与股价背离"意味着一旦AI叙事企稳，该方向可能具备较大的修复弹性；11月的
观察点是费城半导体指数的企稳形态与存储链价格预期（涨价逻辑是否重启）。

### Page 18

18 / 23 
线索四：政策托底链（地产+基建+消费）——前三季度地产投资-20.6%显示基本面
压力仍在；政策端（房贷贴息、现房销售改革扩围至7城）以"精准托底"为主，总量弹
性有限。人大常委会（11月2日-6日）金融法二审与"十五五"开局项目落地节奏是政策
链的观察窗口。该线索的参与价值集中于"政策表态期"的博弈性机会，而非趋势性机会。 
线索五：北交所链（北证50+专精特新）——北交所354家公司三季报披露收官、
37家净利翻倍；北证50月内-14.80%后估值回落（中证指数公司口径市盈率33.66倍），
但增量资金（北证50ETF进度）落地前，修复更可能滞后于主板。该线索属"赔率型"方
向：较低的成交占比与风险度意味着若增量工具落地并带来实际资金流入，相关板块的
修复弹性值得关注。

6.   核心结论与总结 
6.1.   复盘结论

### Page 19

19 / 23 
2026年10月A股的核心特征是"全球AI信用重定价"与"内部拥挤端去化"的共振
深调：万得全A单月-12.44%（2016年1月以来第三大单月跌幅），创业板指-23.02%、
科创50-24.13%（均为历史第二大单月跌幅），通信、电子领跌（-30.23%、-25.70%），
仅防御与能源录得正收益（食品饮料+5.31%、银行+4.84%、石油石化+3.34%）。与9月
加息相关预期带来的估值压力不同，10月美债收益率回落了约35.6个基点、A股却加
速下跌——主导矛盾已从"无风险利率"切换为"AI叙事的信用审视+内部筹码结构"：全
月缩量12.1%（日均成交16097亿元）、两市融资余额下降1833亿元，量价形态属"存
量资金被动降杠杆"而非"恐慌式出逃"。月末（10月26日-29日）五中全会召开、金融
管理部门密集释放"稳市场"信号，指数却收于月内低点——政策底的"信号"与市场底的
"筹码"之间仍有时滞。 
6.2.   展望结论 
2026年11月是"政策维稳+超跌约束"与"外部风险窗口+AI信用冲击"的拉锯，基准
判断为短期震荡修复，中期磨底确认：下有风险度低位（万得全A 2.7%分位）与政策底
封住深度回调空间，上有中选后地缘窗口、12月FOMC悬念与去杠杆未完成约束反弹
斜率。与10月初"顶下移"的修正不同，11月展望的核心修正是"底渐近"：修复的启动需
要"成交回升+AI链企稳"的双确认（详见5.5量化跟踪指标），而模型11月信号-2（历
史上-2样本平均收益-2.09%）提示趋势层面的谨慎仍未解除。结构上，基准情景的占优
方向仍为防御（银行/食品饮料/公用事业）与能源（石油石化/煤炭），超跌成长（电子
/通信）具备赔率、等催化；微盘与北证50属"去化后半程"，反弹弹性弱于主板。 
6.3.   核心预期差清单 
表14：核心预期差清单（2026年11月） 
市场共识 我们的不同判断 
"美债回落将带动A股修复" 
10月数据显示：美债下行35.6bp、A股延续回调；修复条件应换锚为"AI信用冲击
减弱+杠杆去化完成"，美债阈值参考权重下调 
"五中全会维稳信号意味着
政策强刺激" 
全会主题为从严治党与《决定》，金融管理部门的"稳市场"表述属预期管理与制度
性建设，增量刺激需等人大常委会（11月2日-6日）与后续政策窗口确认 
"跌深了就该反弹" 
双创风险度为历史较低分位（1.5%-1.8%分位）提供赔率；胜率确认需要费半企稳+
成交承接。 
"防御/红利已拥挤，行情将
切向成长" 
防御拥挤度抬升属实（66.3%分位），但在"缩量+去杠杆+地缘不确定"的组合下，防
御的相对优势依赖风险偏好收缩；切换的确认信号是风险度回升与成交放量。 
"油价见顶、通胀交易结束" 
油价的地缘溢价由中选后路径决定：打击落地则溢价上冲、斡旋达成或将回吐；通
胀链的定价是"事件驱动"而非"趋势驱动"，需结合11月4日的时间窗口审慎跟踪 
"北证50与微盘已跌透" 
风险度回落至中位但动量较低、月内收于低点；去拥挤的"量"完成大半。反弹的触
发依赖增量资金工具（北证50ETF）与全市场风险偏好修复 
数据来源：Wind, 东吴证券研究所

6.4.   关键跟踪变量

### Page 20

20 / 23 
2026年11月判断的证伪与验证，取决于以下几个可跟踪信号：① 美国中期选举
（11月3日）及选举后的地缘窗口（11月4日前后）——油价与全球风险偏好的分水
岭；② AI信用冲击的减弱信号——费城半导体指数企稳形态、科技巨头信用利差与资
本开支指引变化；③ 两融余额与成交额——去杠杆进程（止跌）与增量资金（1.8万亿
元）的双观察；④ 全国人大常委会（11月2日-6日）——金融法二审与"十五五"开局
政策信号；⑤ 央行"换锚"（11月2日起）后的短端利率与流动性分布——隔夜逆回购
常态化对资金面的实际影响；⑥ 美联储12月会议（12月8日-9日）预期路径——CME
加息概率与美债反应；⑦ 万得全A风险度与微盘短期动量——低位修复与去拥挤结束
的量化确认。 
6.5.   上期观点回顾与验证 
上期《A股9月复盘与10月展望--缩量调整中的结构性分化，与政策托底的十字路
口》对2026年10月作出多项前瞻判断，现以2026年10月实际数据完整回验。 
判断一（10月行情情景）：基准"震荡修复、非单边"，明确否定"延续下跌"。 2026
年10月万得全A实际下跌12.44%（三段式：上旬-4.41%、中旬-2.71%、下旬-5.85%），
为2016年1月以来第三大单月跌幅。判定：错。 偏离归因：低估了"全球AI信用重定
价"的传导强度与高融资余额下"拥挤端集中去化"的负反馈；当时对"缩量制约上行"的判
断方向正确，但把下行风险主要挂在"海外高利率"（实际美债回落）与"12月日历效应"
上，错置了风险来源。可复用的教训：当估值、筹码与叙事三者同时存在脆弱性时，应
按"最坏情景路径"而非"基准路径"准备。 
判断二（结构判断）："微盘股相对承压、超跌成长相对占优"。 2026年10月：万
得微盘股指数-14.74%（跑输万得全A约2.3个百分点）——承压兑现； 创业板指-23.02%、
科创50-24.13%（分别跑输万得全A约10.6、11.7个百分点）——"超跌成长占优"被完
全反向验证。判定：部分对（微盘承压正确、超跌成长占优错误）。 教训："超跌"不是
发动条件，"超跌+催化+筹码出清"才是；在外部信用冲击未减弱时，跌幅越深的方向反
而可能因筹码结构（融资盘、主题基金集中）而继续跑输。 
判断三（行业方向）："政策托底链、超跌修复链占优、资源品承压"。 2026年10
月：政策托底链中银行+4.84%、食品饮料+5.31%（边际上由"托底链"实际演绎为"防御链
"，方向正确）；超跌修复链（电子-25.70%、电力设备-15.88%）方向错误；资源品整体
并未承压——石油石化+3.34%、煤炭+1.29%（能源链走强），仅有色金属-14.62%（工
业金属承压），内部严重分化。判定：部分对（防御方向正确、超跌修复与"资源品承压
"总体错误）。 教训：资源品的定价要看"能源（地缘溢价）"与"工业金属（全球需求）
"的拆分，不能作为整体判断。 
判断四（量化模型）：2026年10月综合信号-1（模型方向判断偏弱）。 万得全A 
10月下跌12.44%，方向兑现、且为-1信号历史上幅度最大的兑现之一。判定：对。 且
本次验证了"The model > 结构直觉"的规律：当模型信号与"政策底、风险度低位"等结构

### Page 21

21 / 23 
信号冲突时，模型侧再次胜出（连续第4个月方向正确）。 
判断五（微盘择时）：2026年10月9日首次触发"局部顶左侧信号"（短期动量由
62.94降至35.69），提示微盘拥挤风险。 此后微盘股于10月15日-16日转向"局部顶
右侧信号"、10月19日起进入"局部顶，右侧信号2"持续状态，全月下跌14.74%、风险
度由106.1回落至73.9。判定：对，且信号链条完整兑现（左侧→右侧→回撤）。 可复
用经验："高拥挤+动量背离（风险度新高而短期动量骤降）"的顶部信号，在外部冲击来
临时兑现速度较快。 
判断六（情景框架）：基准"震荡承压、6000-6400区间"、悲观"下行至5800附近"。 
实际走势跌破基准区间下沿并突破悲观情景的指示位（收于5425.00点）。判定：基准
错、悲观方向对但幅度被突破。 教训：当情景框架的"基准"依赖某一条件（此处为"地
缘不实质升级"）而该条件在月内即被打破（霍尔木兹油轮事件、美方筹备选举后打击）
时，应即时切换至悲观路径跟踪，而非等待月末复盘。 
判断七（风格推演）：红利/价值"由占优转为震荡"、大盘成长"超跌待修复"、微盘
"谨慎"、资源"震荡"。 实际：红利+2.33%、银行+4.84%（防御持续占优，"转震荡"判断
偏保守）；大盘成长方向判断落空、继续回调（同期创业板指-23.02%）；微盘"谨慎"正
确；能源强于"震荡"预期。判定：部分对（微盘、能源判断正确；防御与成长方向偏差）。 
判断八（跟踪阈值）："美债10年期跌破5.0%则压制缓和、成长修复弹性打开"。 
条件实际触发（10月下旬美债下行至4.929%），但A股并未修复、成长加速下跌。判
定：阈值机制失效。 这是本期最重要的方法论修正：A股修复条件的跟踪锚应从"美债
利率"切换为"AI信用冲击减弱（费半/信用利差）+内部杠杆去化（两融/成交）"，美债
与美元降级为背景变量。 
判断九（风险提示）：微盘拥挤度与局部顶风险、地缘冲突升级风险、政策效果不
及预期风险。 三项风险中，微盘与地缘两项均兑现（微盘-14.74%；霍尔木兹油轮事件、
美方筹备选举后打击），政策链风险亦兑现（地产-15.01%）；唯"美联储加息风险"在10
月未兑现（FOMC按兵不动、但12月悬念未解）。判定：风险清单命中率高，但"风险
清单命中"未能转化为"方向判断正确"——风险提示与情景框架之间需要更刚性的联动
机制（本期已在本报告F5情景表中强化，见"触发条件"列）。

7.   风险提示 
1) 美国中期选举后地缘升级风险（概率中、影响高） 
美方被报道筹备在选举后恢复对伊朗的打击（最快11月4日），若落地，原油与
能源价格上行将强化全球再通胀、推高利率中枢并压制风险偏好。跟踪预警指标为霍尔
木兹海峡通航状态、布伦特原油价格（上破125美元则升级确认）与卡塔尔斡旋进展。

### Page 22

22 / 23 
2) 美联储12月加息风险（概率中、影响高） 
10月FOMC按兵不动但两名票委主张加息、12月悬念未解； 若加息超出市场预期，
美债收益率与美元可能面临上行压力。跟踪预警指标为CME加息概率（升破50%则偏
空）与美债10年期收益率（上破5.2%则压制加剧）。 
3) AI信用冲击延续风险（概率中、影响高） 
英特尔、亚马逊等事件显示AI叙事正从"景气定价"转向"信用与回报审视"，若再出
现巨头评级下调、融资受阻或资本开支指引大幅收缩，全球科技股与A股AI链将二次
承压。跟踪预警指标为费城半导体指数、科技巨头信用违约互换报价与资本开支指引。 
4) 去杠杆负反馈风险（概率中、影响中） 
两市融资余额10月下降1833亿元且月末未止跌；若11月上旬出现加速，微盘、
双创等拥挤端可能再度脉冲式下跌。跟踪预警指标为两融余额日度变化、微盘股短期动
量与成交结构。 
5) 政策维稳不及预期风险（概率中、影响中） 
五中全会后金融管理部门的"稳市场"表述若缺乏后续增量工具（流动性、资本市场
制度），政策底的预期支撑将衰减。跟踪预警指标为人大常委会审议内容与证监会后续
举措、成交额变化。 
6) 数据口径风险（概率低、影响低） 
行业/风格/指数涨跌为原始数据2026年9月30日至2026年10月30日实算；风险
度为因子数据 TR（各指数自成立以来分位）；美债收益率为美国财政部日度收盘口径
（2026年10月30日：10年期4.929%）；全球资产表现部分采用市场收盘与媒体转述
口径（已在表5标注）。海外市场与事件信息以公开报道为准，单一来源表述已标注。

Economy 
COLA comfort for older consumers? 
27 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Older generations' spending growth has lagged other cohorts over the last year in Bank of America aggregated credit and debit 
card data, despite sizeable wealth gains. This likely partly reflects the highly uneven distribution of wealth among older 
households, limiting the extent to which rising wealth supports spending across the cohort. 
• More recently, stronger consumer spending appears to be primarily a labor-income story. However, older households are less 
exposed to accelerating wage growth. In addition, large differences in their income sources help explain the considerable 
variation in spending among this cohort. 
• The recently announced 3.6% Social Security cost-of-living adjustment could provide a meaningful boost in 2027. If inflation 
moderates as expected, benefit increases may outpace price growth, supporting older households' purchasing power and 
potentially narrowing their spending gap with other generations.

Older generations’ spending growth has lagged  
While total credit and debit card spending per household has picked up in 2026 in Bank of America internal data, a notable 
laggard has been the spending growth of older generations (Baby Boomers and Traditionalists). For around a year, discretionary 
spending growth for these older generations has been lower than that of overall households. This is in stark contrast to 2022-
2023, when they were significantly outstripping overall household spending growth (Exhibit 1).  
Exhibit 1: Discretionary spending growth has been slower among older (Baby Boomer and Traditionalist) households despite wealth effects 
Total credit and debit card spending per household on discretionary categories by older generations and overall (three-month average, % YoY)

Source: Bank of America internal data

On one level, this observation seems surprising given that in our piece on the older consumer we noted that a large and growing 
proportion of net worth is held by older cohorts (read: Entering the age of the older consumer). So what’s going on? One 
explanation is that wealth is very unevenly distributed even within the older cohort. Exhibit 2 uses the latest Federal Reserve 
(2025) Survey of Consumer Finances to illustrate that, across households older than 65 years of age, around two-thirds of net 
wealth is held by the wealthiest 10% of households. As a result, wealth gains from rising stock markets may not trickle too far 
down the wealth distribution of older consumers.  
Exhibit 2: The wealth distribution for older households is very skewed  
Distribution of net wealth for households over 65 years old (%)

Source: Federal Reserve Board of Governors (2025 Survey of Consumer Finances)  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Income and spending become more diverse and unequal with age 
Another important part of the story is that the rise in overall spending growth over the past year appears to be supported by 
labor market strength as well as rising wealth. The October Consumer Checkpoint described how a pickup in after-tax wage 
growth among lower- and middle-income households was likely a key factor driving a recovery in these cohorts’ spending growth 
(read: Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers). As households age, their income sources become more diverse (Exhibit 3), 
which means older generations will likely benefit less from this pickup in wage growth.  
Exhibit 3: As households age their sources of income become more diverse  
Sources of income by age (%)

Source: Federal Reserve Board of Governors (2025 Survey of Household Economics and Decision making) 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
The greater diversity in income sources among older households helps explain the wide dispersion in their total incomes and 
spending. For example, using Bank of America internal data, Exhibit 4 shows that households over age 62 that rely solely on

The 2027 COLA may offer some respite  
Still in our view there are some reasons for optimism around older generations spending outcomes.  
For some older households in the middle of the income distribution, tax policy is likely providing some support to their spending 
growth. The One Big Beautiful Bill Act introduced a temporary deduction of up to $6,000 for each taxpayer aged 65 or older for 
2025-2028, with the benefit phasing out above $75,000 of modified adjusted gross income for single filers and $150,000 for 
joint filers. This measure is likely lifting after-tax income and spending capacity for older households – particularly those in the 
middle of the income distribution.  
However, households on lower incomes may see less benefit from these policies as they likely already owe limited federal 
income tax. For these households relying on Social Security either as their sole or as a large component of their income, the 
forthcoming annual cost-of-living adjustment (COLA) to social security benefits will likely be a more important determinant of 
their budgets for 2027. The announced COLA for 2027 is a rise of 3.6% (Exhibit 7) – the largest since 2023, though lower than 
the 8.7% rise in 2022. 
Exhibit 7: The 2027 COLA increase is the largest since 2023 and close to the historical average  
Social Security cost of living adjustments (% increase as of January effective date)

Source: US Social Security Administration (SSA)   
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
The COLA increases are based on the year-over-year (YoY) % change between the third quarters of successive years in the 
Consumer Price Index for Urban Wage Earners and Clerical Workers (CPI-W) and are typically announced in October and effective 
the following January. If the COLA increase simply keeps benefits in line with inflation, why is it particularly interesting? For sure, 
many times it is not. But when inflation is close to a turning point, the increase can end up looking relatively high or low 
compared with inflation over the course of the following year. The 2022 COLA increase was a case in point, as inflation in the 
subsequent year slowed sharply. In our view, this was one reason older generations’ spending growth was stronger than that of 
other age cohorts over 2022-2023.   
Exhibit 8: Inflation is expected to decline next year 
Consumer Price Index (CPI) (% YoY), third quarter CPI-W increases (red dots, % YoY) and latest consensus CPI forecast for 2027 (dotted line, % YoY)

Source: Haver Analytics, Bloomberg. Some fall 2025 months might be missing due to the government shutdown.  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Regarding the 2027 COLA announcement, there are some parallels with 2022, albeit on a smaller scale. In particular, the 
consensus expectation is that CPI inflation is likely to cool over 2027, largely reflecting the lessening impact of higher gasoline 
prices (Exhibit 8).  
So if these expectations prove correct, households that rely on Social Security may receive a more impactful boost to their 
budgets next year than they have for the last few, potentially narrowing the gap between older generations’ spending and other 
households.  
What categories of spending growth might benefit from this? It is hard to be sure, but notable growth deficits for older 
generations currently reside in discretionary areas like electronics, lodging and restaurants (Exhibit 9). Some of these may reflect 
the preferences of older households, but it could be that there is room for some narrowing in these areas.  
Exhibit 9: Large spending growth gaps for older households exist in a range of discretionary goods and services 
Difference between older generations’ (baby boomer and traditionalist) spending and overall households’ YoY spending growth by category (three-month 
moving average, percentage points)

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
The differences between the total and per household card spending growth rate (if discussed) can be explained by the following reasons:  
1. Overall total card spending growth is partially boosted by the growth in the number of active cardholders in our sample. This could be due to 
an increasing customer base or inactive customers using their cards more frequently. 
2. Per household card spending growth only looks at households that complete at least five transactions with Bank of America cards in the 
month. Per household spending growth isolates impacts from a changing sample size, which could be unrelated to underlying economic 
momentum, and potential spending volatility from less active users. 
3. Overall total card spending includes small business card spending while per household card spending does not. 
4. Differences due to using processing dates (total card spending) versus transaction date (per household card spending). 
5. Other differences including household formations due to young adults moving in and out of their parent’s houses during COVID. 
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards are excluded. Data 
regarding merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial 
services companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System 
(NAICS), which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
Store category aggregation for “more discretionary”, “partly discretionary”, and “mostly necessity” is defined as follows: 
More discretionary: furniture, electronics, building materials, hobbies, miscellaneous stores, nonstore retail, clothing. 
Partly discretionary: auto, health and personal care, general merchandise. 
Mostly necessity: Gasoline, food and beverage.  
We consider a measure of services necessity spending that includes but is not limited to groceries, utilities, and gasoline. Discretionary services 
includes but is not limited to charitable donations, leisure travel, entertainment, and professional/consumer services. Discretionary retail 
includes but is not limited to general merchandise, miscellaneous, clothing, electronics and furniture. It excludes categories like groceries and 
gasoline. Holiday spending is defined as items in which spending in the November-December period is usually at least 20% of total annual 
spending on the category.  Value and premium grocers are determined judgmentally on a proprietary analysis of merchants by equity analysts. 
The “more discretionary” retail category includes furniture, electronics, building materials, online marketplaces, clothing retailers and 
miscellaneous stores; while “partly discretionary” retail contains auto parts and services, health and personal care retailers, general merchandise 
and “mostly necessity”  retail contains gas and groceries. 
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Bank of America Proprietary Market Landscape Insights Study is an online quantitative survey among Bank of America customers and 
noncustomers sampled and balanced to provide a representative view of the U.S. adult population. Insights are based on aggregated and 
anonymized responses to surveys. Significance testing is done at the 90 percent confidence interval. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Document 355: How bad is the rise in bond yields_ (2026-09-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/How bad is the rise in bond yields_ (2026-09-23).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
